<analysis>
<original_problem_statement>
The user wants to build a mobile app for tattoo artists named BODY BOUND stencil generator. The core function is to convert a realistic photo into a simplified line drawing/stencil.

**PRODUCT REQUIREMENTS**:
1.  **Photo to Stencil Conversion**: Convert photos to stencils with a specific style (bold outlines, dotted/dashed contour lines for shading).
2.  **AI Reliability & Quality**: The system must be fast and reliable. Recent work focused on improving AI output quality via pre/post-processing and validation.
3.  **UI/UX**: The app should have a dark luxury theme and a maximized image preview area.
4.  **Save/Export**: Save original (JPEG) and stencil (transparent PNG) with identical dimensions for perfect alignment in Procreate.
5.  **Stencil Editing (Procreate-style)**:
    *   Drawing must be possible **only with an Apple Pencil on iPad**. Finger touches should be for navigation (pan/zoom).
    *   Pinch-to-zoom and two-finger pan functionality.
6.  **Stencil Alignment**: The generated stencil must perfectly align with the reference photo. This is a recurring issue.
7.  **Image Quality Validation**: The frontend should call the backend's  endpoint and display warnings to the user if an image is of poor quality (blurry, low-res, etc.).
8.  **Deployment**: The application must be deployable to production. The deployment is currently failing.
</original_problem_statement>
